Detailed plan for final code:
Top-level globals:
```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
_GLOBAL = {}
```
But no closures; using dict okay.

Functions:
- `fast_conv(a)` returning np array length 2n-1.
- `compute_score(a)` internal, normalize? It should compute exact score for a not necessarily sum1. Use S; if S<0.01 return inf (like eval).

Now, let's design LP polish in more detail for performance:
```
def lp_polish(a, max_rows=None, rho=0.05, t_end=None, max_rounds=1):
    a=normalize(a)
    n=len(a)
    best=a; best_sc=compute_score(a)
    for _ in range(max_rounds):
       if t_end and time.monotonic()>t_end: break
       c=fast_conv(a); cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax <=0: break
       k = min(max_rows or 120, 2*n-1)
       active=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k]
       A=np.zeros((k,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
       b=np.empty(k)
       for ii,kk in enumerate(active):
           kk=int(kk)
           ist=max(0, kk-n+1); ied=min(kk,n-1)
           A[ii, ist:ied+1] = 2.0 * a[kk-ist:kk-ied-1:-1]
           b[ii]=cmax-c[kk]+1e-12
       A[-? no, separate equality]
       eq=np.ones(n+1); eq[-1]=0
       c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=-1.0
       scale=np.mean(a)  # 1/n
       low=-rho*a
       high=rho*np.maximum(a, scale)  # allow small positive at zeros
       bounds=list(zip(low, high))
       bounds.append((0.0, 0.25*cmax))  # eta
       try:
           res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=eq, b_eq=[0.0], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'maxiter':5000})
       except Exception:
           break
       if not res.success: continue
       eta=float(res.x[-1]); delta=res.x[:n]
       if eta <= 1e-10: continue
       # line search
       improved=False
       for alpha in (1.0,0.7,0.4,0.2,0.1):
           a_new= a + alpha*delta
           # ensure nonneg
           if a_new.min() < -1e-12: continue
           a_new=np.clip(a_new,0,1000)
           S=a_new.sum()
           if S<0.01: continue
           a_new/=S
           sc=compute_score(a_new)
           if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-7):
               best_sc=sc; best=a_new; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           # maybe try alpha=eta/??? no
          pass
       a=best
    return best,best_sc
```
But the sum equality in LP uses δ sum 0; line search renormalizes. Good.
Potential: `high=rho*np.maximum(a, scale)` if a_i zero, high=rho*scale. For n=8192 scale=1.2e-4, high=6e-6, total allowed increase 0.05. Good. low=-rho*a, no lower for zeros.
We need include eta in A_ub? We forgot A[:, -1]=1. Set in A initialization and for each row last column 1.

_CALLBACK_DEADLINE = 0.0
def obj_general(x):

    else:
        return weighted_score_grad(x, _OPT['w'])
def cb_general(x):
    return time.monotonic() > _CALLBACK_DEADLINE
def run_lbfgs(a0, mode, param, maxiter=4000, maxfun=20000, t_end=None):
    a=np.clip(a0,0,1000).astype(float); norm...
    _OPT['mode']=mode; _OPT['beta']=param if mode lse; _OPT['w']=param
    _CALLBACK_DEADLINE = t_end if t_end else np.inf
    bounds=[(0,1000)]*n
    try: res=minimize(obj_general,a,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,callback=cb_general,options={...maxiter...})
    except: return a
    out=np.clip(res.x,0,1000); normalize
    return out
```

def plateau_sqrt_init(n, target_score, floor=0.02, taper_frac=0.1):
  M=...
  Lsup=2*n-1; center=n-1
  L = int(round(2*n / max(1e-6,target_score)))
  L=min(max(L,1),Lsup)
  idx=np.arange(Lsup); dist=abs(idx-center); half=0.5*L
  w=max(1,int(0.2*(Lsup-L)))
  q=np.zeros(Lsup)
  mask=dist<=half; q[mask]=1
  taper=(dist>half)&(dist<=half+w); q[taper]=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*(dist[taper]-half)/w)
  q += floor
  q/=q.sum()
  qp=np.zeros(M); qp[:Lsup]=q
  B=np.fft.fft(qp)
  A=np.sqrt(B)
  a=np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
  a=np.maximum(a,0)
  if sum<... uniform
  return a
```

def get_fft_len(a):
    n=len(a); return 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    M=1 << max(1, (2*n-2).bit_length())
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)
    return b[:2*n-1]
```
Use full? rfft returns len M//2+1, irfft with M. Good.

`compute_score`:
```
def compute_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    if cmax <0: cmax=0
    return 2.0*len(a)*cmax/(S*S)
```

`normalize`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
    return a/S
```

`interpolate_sequence`: If source length 1? Use np.interp; ensure endpoints. If n small. Clip.

a=a.copy()
noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
if lowpass: F=np.fft.rfft(noise); F[?]=0 for high; noise=np.fft.irfft(F,M, n?);
a = a + strength*noise
a=np.maximum(a,0)
return normalize
```
Since no closures, top-level.

Plateau target:
```
def plateau_target_array(n,target_score,floor=0.03,taper_frac=0.15):
    Lsup=2*n-1; c=n-1
    ts=max(1.01,target_score)
    L=int(round(2.0*n/ts))
    L=max(3,min(L,Lsup))
    idx=np.arange(Lsup)
    dist=np.abs(idx-c)
    half=0.5*L
    q=np.zeros(Lsup)
    mask=dist<=half; q[mask]=1
    w=int(max(1,taper_frac*max(1,Lsup-L)))
    taper=(dist>half)&(dist<=half+w)
    if w>0: q[taper]=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*(dist[taper]-half)/w)
    q=max? q+=floor
    q/=q.sum()
    return q
```
If floor raises max; okay.

arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
if sum: normalize
# also try abs
arr2=np.abs(np.fft.ifft(A))[:n]; arr2=np.maximum(arr2,0);...
return [arr, arr2]?
```

H=np.sqrt(B)
# force H[k]=conj(H[M-k])? But B[k]=conj(B[M-k]). Principal sqrt may differ; set H = 0.5*(H + conj(H_flip))? 
H2=np.zeros_like(H); H2[0]=H[0].real; H2[M//2 if even]=H[M//2].real; for k in 1..M//2: H2[k]=0.5*(H[k]+conj(H[M-k])); H2[M-k]=conj(H2[k])
```
This might produce a consistent square root with real ifft. Let's implement a top-level `hermitian_sqrt_fourier(B)` maybe. But costly. Simpler: use full FFT and real part. The real part may have negative values; clipping okay. To improve, after ifft, if many negative, use abs version.

```
def gs_plateau(a, target_score, t_end, n_iters_max=300, alpha_init=0.6, alpha_final=0.05):
    a=normalize; n; M
    q=plateau_target_array(n,target_score)
    qp=zeros M; qp[:2n-1]=q
    T=np.fft.fft(qp); T_mag=abs; T_phase=angle
    curr=compute_score? Maybe compute once
    best=a; best_sc
    for it in range(n_iters_max):
       if time.monotonic()>t_end: break
       prog=...
       alpha=...
       A=np.fft.fft(a,M); A_mag=max(abs(A),1e-30); A_phase=np.angle(A)
       new_mag = A_mag**(1-alpha) * T_mag**(0.5*alpha)
       new_phase = (1-alpha)*A_phase + 0.5*alpha*T_phase
       A_new=new_mag*np.exp(1j*new_phase)
       a_new=np.fft.ifft(A_new,M).real[:n]
       a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
       if a_new.sum()<1e-12: break
       a_new/=a_new.sum()
       a=a_new
       if it%5==0 or it==last:
          sc=compute_score(a); if sc<best_sc: update
    sc=...
    return best
```
But T_phase may have discontinuities; ok.
For GS with target, if T_mag has zeros, log? We use power not log, so if T_mag=0, new_mag=0. Need T_mag max(0). If T_mag zero, new_mag zero; A_mag min 1e-30 prevents. Good.

`waterfill_target(c, clip_ratio=0.98, floor=0.02)`:
```
cmax=c.max(); R=clip_ratio*cmax
q=np.minimum(c,R)
rem=1-q.sum()
if rem>0:
  low=q<R
  if low.sum()==0: q += rem/c.size
  else:
     # iterative fill
     for _ in range(20):
       if rem<=1e-14: break
       mask=q<R-1e-15
       if not mask.any(): break
       space=R-q
       add=rem/mask.sum()
       add=np.minimum(add, space[mask])? Uniform loop slow. Simpler:
       # choose lowest capacity: use idx sorted by q? Use water fill:
       order=np.argsort(q[mask])
       available = space[order]
       for idx in order:
          if rem<=0 break
          cap=R-q[idx]
          d=min(cap, rem)
          q[idx]+=d; rem-=d
# Then floor
q += floor
q /= q.sum()
```

```

```

```
def waterfill_target(c, clip_ratio=0.98, floor=0.0):
  c=np.asarray(c)
  cmax=float(c.max()); R=max(clip_ratio*cmax,1e-300)
  q=np.minimum(c,R)
  rem=float(1-q.sum())
  if rem>0:
     idx=np.argsort(q)
     for i in idx:
       if rem<=1e-15: break
       d=min(R-q[i], rem)
       if d>0: q[i]+=d; rem-=d
  if rem>1e-12:
     q += rem/q.size
  q += floor
  q /= max(q.sum(),1e-300)
  return q
```
Good.

Now, code for `run_lbfgs` time:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0, mode, param, maxiter=3000, maxfun=15000, t_end=None):
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
    _OPT['mode']=mode
    if mode=='lse': _OPT['beta']=float(param); _OPT['w']=None
    else: _OPT['w']=np.asarray(param,dtype=np.float64)
    if t_end is None: _STATE['deadline']=np.inf
    else: _STATE['deadline']=t_end
    n=len(a)
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    try:
       res=minimize(obj_general,a, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, callback=cb_general, options={'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-11,'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'maxls':20})
       x=res.x
    except Exception:
       return a
    x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    S=float(x.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return a
    return x/S
```
If t_end is None, deadline inf; if stage passed, L-BFGS won't stop unless maxiter. We'll always pass t_end.

Weighted objective requires w length 2n-1. If param None, error but catch.

But LSE gradient with all nonpositive c-cmax: max_scaled 0; exp_s sum at least1. Good. Score = cmax + logsumexp(beta(c-cmax))/beta. If beta huge and no underflow, ~cmax. Good.

def iterative_reweighting_best(a0, t_end, max_outers=20, chunk_s=120.0):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    best=a; best_sc=compute_score(a)
    n=len(a)
    for outer in range(max_outers):
       if time.monotonic() > t_end: break
       c=fast_conv(a); cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax<=0: break
       # temp schedule: start cmax/3 to cmax/50
       temp = cmax * max(0.02, 0.33/(1+0.5*outer))? Actually outer increases -> smaller. Use exp decay: temp = cmax * 0.3 * (0.7**outer) + 1e-30
       w=np.exp((c-cmax)/temp)
       w += 1e-6
       w /= w.sum()
       w = 0.05/(2n-1) + 0.95*w; normalize
       inner_end = min(t_end, time.monotonic()+chunk_s)
       a = run_lbfgs(a, 'weighted', w, maxiter=1000, maxfun=5000, t_end=inner_end)
       a=normalize_sequence(a)
       sc=compute_score(a)
       if sc<best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; print
    return best,best_sc
```

Perturbation function:
```
def perturbed_restart(a, rng, strength=0.05):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    n=len(a)
    noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
    # lowpass to avoid high-freq causing nonneg issues
    M=1<< (2*n-2).bit_length()
    F=np.fft.rfft(noise, M)
    cutoff=int(len(F)*0.2)
    F[cutoff:]=0
    noise=np.fft.irfft(F,M)[:n]
    a += strength*noise
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```
If all zero, return a0.

Let's code `lse_score_grad`:
```
def lse_score_grad(x, beta):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(x)
    c=fast_conv(x)
    S=float(np.sum(x))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    maxs=float(np.max(scaled))
    exps=np.exp(scaled-maxs)
    lse_l = cmax + (maxs + float(np.log(np.sum(exps))))/beta
    p=exps/float(np.sum(exps))
    rev=x[::-1]
    M2=3*n-2
    M2f=1 << max(1,(M2-1).bit_length())
    RA=np.fft.rfft(rev,M2f); P=np.fft.rfft(p,M2f)
    conv_res=np.fft.irfft(RA*P,M2f)[n-1:2*n-1]
    g=2.0*conv_res
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    grad=(2.0*n)*(g*S - 2.0*lse_l)/S3
    score=2.0*n*lse_l/S2
    return float(score), grad
```
If n=0? not.
 `maxs` should be 0 or positive due numerical; if cmax is max, c-cmax<=0 -> maxs=0; but due rounding maybe small; ok.
If beta huge and p all underflow to zeros except one, sum >0.

def obj_general(x):

        sc,g = weighted_score_grad(x, _OPT['w'])
    else:
        sc,g = lse_score_grad(x, _OPT['beta'])
    if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=1e10
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g=np.zeros_like(x)
    return sc, g
```

`waterfill_target`: Input c maybe length 2n-1 sum1. If c sum not exactly1 due float, normalize first? It should be sum S² if sum a not1. In GS, a normalized sum1, conv sum=1. But for safety: `c = c / max(c.sum(),1e-300)`. Then cmax. Good.
Waterfill algorithm:
```
def waterfill_target(c, clip_ratio=0.98, floor=0.0):
   c=np.asarray(c,float).copy()
   tot=c.sum()
   if tot<=0: return np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
   c/=tot
   cmax=c.max(); R=clip_ratio*cmax
   if R<=0: return c
   q=np.minimum(c,R)
   rem=float(1-q.sum())
   if rem>0:
      idx=np.argsort(q)
      for i in idx:
         if rem<=1e-15: break
         cap=R-q[i]
         if cap>0:
            d=cap if cap<rem else rem
            q[i]+=d; rem-=d
   if rem>1e-14:
      q += rem/q.size
   if floor>0:
      q += floor
   s=q.sum()
   if s>0: q/=s
   return q
```
If floor raises above R, okay. If clip_ratio >1? We won't. If clip_ratio=0, R=0, rem=1, waterfill with R=0 none, uniform add -> uniform target. okay.

support=2*n-1
q=np.asarray(q,float).ravel()
if q.size != support:
   tmp=np.zeros(support); L=min(len(q),support); tmp[:L]=q[:L]; q=tmp
if q.sum()<=0: q=np.ones(support)/support
```

`lp_polish` details:
```
def lp_polish(a0, max_rows=100, max_rounds=3, rho=0.1, t_end=None):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    n=len(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    for r in range(max_rounds):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if t_end is not None and now > t_end: break
       c=fast_conv(a); cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax <=0 or not finite: break
       Lsup=len(c)
       # K cap
       K = max(20, min(max_rows, Lsup))
       if n > 10000: K = min(K,80)
       elif n > 5000: K = min(K,120)
       # active selection
       order=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
       top=order[:max(5,min(K//2,Lsup))]
       high_mask=c >= 0.97*cmax
       high_idx=np.where(high_mask)[0]
       if high_idx.size > K - top.size:
           step=max(1, high_idx.size // max(1, K-top.size))
           sampled=high_idx[::step][:K-top.size]
           active=np.unique(np.concatenate((top, sampled)))[:K]
       else:
           active=np.concatenate((top, high_idx))
           active=np.unique(active)[:K]
       # If still <K, add more order
       if active.size < K:
           extra=order[active.size:K]
           # unique maybe
           active=np.unique(np.concatenate((active, extra)))[:K]
       Klen=active.size
       A=np.zeros((Klen,n+1), dtype=float)
       b=np.empty(Klen)
       slack=1e-10*cmax + 1e-15
       for ii,kk in enumerate(active):
           kk=int(kk)
           ist=max(0, kk-n+1); ied=min(kk,n-1)
           A[ii, ist:ied+1] = 2.0 * a[kk-ist: kk-ied-1 : -1]
           A[ii, -1] = 1.0
           b[ii] = max(0.0, cmax - c[kk]) + slack
       ceq=np.ones(n+1); ceq[-1]=0.0
       cobj=np.zeros(n+1); cobj[-1]=-1.0
       scale=float(np.mean(a))
       lo=-rho*a
       hi=rho*(a + 0.5*scale) + 1e-12
       # For zero a, hi small; maybe also add rho*scale? already +0.5 scale.
       bounds=[]
       for i in range(n):
           bounds.append((float(lo[i]), float(hi[i])))
       bounds.append((0.0, float(0.25*cmax)))
       try:
          res=linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=ceq, b_eq=[0.0], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'primal_tolerance':1e-10,'dual_tolerance':1e-12,'gap_tol':1e-10,'maxiter':10000})
       except Exception:
          break
       if not res.success:
          continue
       eta=float(res.x[-1]); delta=res.x[:n]
       if eta <= 1e-12:
          continue
       improved=False
       for alpha in (1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1):
          a_new=a + alpha*delta
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)): continue
          if a_new.min() < -1e-10: continue
          a_new=np.clip(a_new,0.0,1000.0)
          S=float(a_new.sum())
          if S < 0.01: continue
          a_new/=S
          sc=compute_score(a_new)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc*(1-1e-9):
             best_sc=sc; best=a_new.copy(); improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          # try a smaller alpha maybe eta scaled
          for alpha in (0.05,0.02,0.01):
             a_new=a + alpha*delta
             ... same
       a=best
       if t_end is not None and time.monotonic() > t_end: break
    return best,best_sc
```

Now, `perturbed_restart`:
```
def perturbed_restart(a0, rng, strength=0.05):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    n=len(a)
    noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
    M=1 << max(1,(2*n-2).bit_length())
    F=np.fft.rfft(noise,M)
    cut=max(2,int(len(F)*0.25))
    F[cut:]=0.0
    noise=np.fft.irfft(F,M)[:n]
    noise = noise - noise.mean()
    a = a + strength*noise
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if a.sum()<1e-12: return a0.copy()
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```

`make_sparse_inits`: Maybe not needed, but okay. Need ensure sum >0. Use `rename?`.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0 + max(60.0, budget - 60.0)
    parent=None; parent_score=np.inf
    try:
       if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
          p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
          if p.size>=16:
             parent=p
             ps=compute_score(p)
             if np.isfinite(ps): parent_score=ps
    except Exception: pass
    # choose n
    candidate_ns=[]
    if parent is not None:
       L=parent.size
       candidate_ns.append(L)
       candidate_ns.append(8192)
       if 0 < L < 8192:
          candidate_ns.append(min(8192, L+1000))
       candidate_ns.append(6962)
    else:
       candidate_ns=[8192,6962,7500]
    # unique, filter 64..12000 maybe
    unique=[]
    for n in candidate_ns:
       if 64 <= n <= 12000 and n not in unique:
          unique.append(n)
    if not unique: unique=[8192]
    # evaluate interpolated parent or random init for each
    best_n=None; best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for n in unique:
       if parent is not None:
          a=interpolate_sequence(parent,n)
       else:
          a=make_fourier_inits(n,rng,1)[0]
       sc=compute_score(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          # adjusted prefer larger n slightly
          adj=sc - 0.004*(float(n)/8192.0)
          if adj < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_n=n; best_a=a; best_sc=sc
    if best_a is None:
       best_n=unique[0]; best_a=normalize_sequence(np.ones(best_n))/best_n; best_sc=compute_score(best_a)
    n=best_n
    # Now maybe best_a score internal; store fallback
    fallback_a = best_a.copy(); fallback_sc=best_sc
    # If parent original score lower than fallback, maybe consider separately?
    best_a=normalize_sequence(best_a); best_sc=compute_score(best_a)
    # output stage times
    usable=deadline-t0
    stage1=t0 + 0.07*usable
    stage2=t0 + 0.25*usable
    stage3=t0 + 0.50*usable
    stage4=t0 + 0.85*usable
    # print initial
```

Let's inspect potential syntax pitfalls:
- Slicing reverse `a[kk-ist: kk-ied-1 : -1]`: If kk-ied-1 < kk-ist, works. Example kk=n-1, ist=0, ied=n-1, stop=-1 -> from n-1 down to0 inclusive? `a[n-1:-1:-1]` stops at 0 exclusive -1, includes0. Good. kk=0, ist=0, ied=0, stop= -1? kk-ied-1=-1; `a[0:-1:-1]`? start=0, stop=-1, step=-1: In Python, start 0, stop -1 step -1: if stop is -1 (i.e., 0), slice is empty! This is a bug for kk=0! Need compute stop index carefully. For reverse slice from start=s to stop=stop_exclusive, step -1. We want include index e=kk-ied. Since ied-ist length. For kk=0, ist=0, ied=0, want a[0: -? : -1] to include 0. Stop should be -1? For a[0:-1:-1] stop index 0? In Python negative -1 is last, for step -1, condition while index > stop. stop=-1 means index > -1, so index0 included? Let's test mentally: `range(0, -1, -1)` yields 0. But slice with stop=-1 and step -1: a[0:-1:-1] includes index 0? Actually `a[3:-1:-1]` from 3 down to0 includes 0 because stop -1 is before 0. For start0 stop -1 step -1, index0 > -1 yes include? It might yield [0]. Let's check Python list: [1,2,3][0:-1:-1] -> [1]? I think yes? Actually [0:-1] with positive step excludes last; with negative step, stop -1 means last index, not included? Let's be sure: `a[0:-1:-1]` start0, step -1, stop -1 => index sequence 0; next -1 which equals stop? stop interpreted as -1 absolute last; condition i > stop? For negative step, if i > stop, next; i=0, stop=-1 => 0 > -1 true, include. Then i=-1, i > -1 false. So includes 0. Good. But if list empty? not. Another test [1,2][0:-1:-1] -> [1]. okay. For kk=2,n=3, ist=0,ied=2, start=2, stop=2-2-1=-1 => a[2:-1:-1] gives indices2,1,0, good. So formula ok. But when stop equals -1 and start 0, includes 0. Good.
For kk=1,n=2 ist=0,ied=1,start=1,stop= -1 => a[1:-1:-1] indices1,0. good.
If stop computed negative other than -1, okay? e.g., kk=1,n=5, ist=0,ied=1,start=1,stop=-1, indices1,0. If kk=1, ied=1. good. If kk=2,n=5, ist=0,ied=2,start=2,stop=-1, indices2,1,0. ```python
"""
Improve a non-negative step-function coefficient sequence for the quadratic
convolution-peak objective.

Main ingredients:
- Use the previous construction when available, but also re-optimise at nearby
  lengths (especially up to 8192, which still uses the same FFT length).
- Spectral initialisations from flat "plateau" convolution targets and from
  sqrt(FFT(target)) phase projections.
- Alternating projections / GS iterations with adaptive water-filled targets
  that pull the largest convolution peaks down and spread mass to lower peaks.
- L-BFGS on a log-sum-exp smoothed objective and on weighted peak objectives.
- LP-based linearised polish: locally solve a linear program that maximises a
  guaranteed first-order reduction of a spatially sampled set of near-maximal
  convolution peaks, then accept by exact line search.
- Perturbed restarts to escape shallow local minima.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

_OPT = {'mode': 'lse', 'beta': 1000.0, 'w': None}
_STATE = {'deadline': np.inf}


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution via zero-padded real FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    M = 1 << max(1, (2 * n - 2).bit_length())
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)
    return b[:2 * n - 1]


def compute_score(a):
    """Internal exact score matching the evaluation function up to scaling."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))


def normalize_sequence(a):
    """Clip to nonnegative finite range and scale to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(a.shape, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / S


def interpolate_sequence(a, n):
    """Upsample/downsample a 1D array to length n, preserving sum."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if len(a) == n:
        return a.copy()
    if len(a) == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a.sum()) / n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(a))
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sequence(y)


def plateau_target_array(n, target_score, floor=0.03, taper_frac=0.18):
    """
    Build a normalised convolution target with a plateau of roughly
    1/target_score of the full length.  The target has sum 1 and the centre
    is at k=n-1.
    """
    Lsup = 2 * n - 1
    center = n - 1
    ts = max(1.05, float(target_score))
    L = int(round(2.0 * n / ts))
    L = max(3, min(L, Lsup))

    idx = np.arange(Lsup, dtype=np.float64)
    dist = np.abs(idx - center)
    half = 0.5 * L
    q = np.zeros(Lsup, dtype=np.float64)

    q[dist <= half] = 1.0
    taper = max(1, int(taper_frac * max(1, Lsup - L)))
    mask = (dist > half) & (dist <= half + taper)
    if mask.any():
        q[mask] = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * (dist[mask] - half) / float(taper))

    q = q + float(floor)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        q = np.ones(Lsup, dtype=np.float64) / float(Lsup)
    else:
        q /= s
    return q


def sqrt_target_init(n, target_score, floor=0.03):
    """
    A crude but useful initialisation: take a plateau convolution target,
    compute sqrt of its FFT, try several global phase shifts, and keep the
    most positive real projection.
    """
    Lsup = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1 << max(1, (2 * n - 2).bit_length())

    q = plateau_target_array(n, target_score, floor=floor)
    qp = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    qp[:Lsup] = q

    B = np.fft.fft(qp)
    baseA = np.sqrt(B)

    best = None
    best_sum = -1.0
    phases = (0.0, 0.25 * np.pi, -0.25 * np.pi, 0.5 * np.pi,
              -0.5 * np.pi, np.pi, -np.pi / 3.0, np.pi / 3.0)
    for ph in phases:
        A = baseA * np.exp(1j * ph)
        arr = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n].copy()
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s > best_sum:
            best_sum = s
            best = arr

    arr_abs = np.abs(np.fft.ifft(baseA))[:n].copy()
    s_abs = float(arr_abs.sum())
    if s_abs > best_sum:
        best = arr_abs

    if best is None or best_sum <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return normalize_sequence(best)


def make_plateau_inits(n):
    out = []
    for ts in (1.5, 1.6, 1.8, 2.1):
        out.append(sqrt_target_init(n, ts))
    return out


def make_fourier_inits(n, rng, count=10):
    out = []
    if n <= 0:
        return out
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    for _ in range(count):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        n_modes = int(rng.integers(4, 26))
        amp0 = float(rng.uniform(0.18, 0.55))
        decay = float(rng.uniform(0.7, 1.3))
        for m in range(1, n_modes + 1):
            w = amp0 / (float(m) ** decay)
            ph = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
            a += w * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            a += float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.18)) * np.cos(np.pi * x)
        a += 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-3)
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        if a.size == n and np.isfinite(compute_score(a)):
            out.append(a)
    return out


def make_sparse_inits(n, rng, count=2):
    out = []
    if n <= 0:
        return out
    for d in (0.45, 0.65):
        mask = rng.random(n) < d
        if int(mask.sum()) < max(2, n // 20):
            continue
        vals = np.abs(rng.normal(1.0, 0.45, size=n)) + 0.05
        a = np.where(mask, vals, 0.0).astype(np.float64)
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        out.append(a)
    return out


def perturbed_restart(a0, rng, strength=0.04):
    """Low-pass random perturbation of a normalised sequence."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    M = 1 << max(1, (2 * n - 2).bit_length())
    F = np.fft.rfft(noise, M)
    cut = max(2, int(len(F) * 0.25))
    F[cut:] = 0.0
    noise = np.fft.irfft(F, M)[:n]
    noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
    a = a + strength * noise
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.sum() <= 0.0:
        return a0
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def build_initial_candidates(n, parent, rng):
    inits = []
    if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        p = normalize_sequence(parent)
        inits.append(interpolate_sequence(p, n))
        ps = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
        inits.append(interpolate_sequence(ps, n))
        inits.append(perturbed_restart(interpolate_sequence(p, n), rng, 0.02))
        inits.append(perturbed_restart(interpolate_sequence(p, n), rng, 0.05))

    inits.extend(make_plateau_inits(n))
    inits.extend(make_fourier_inits(n, rng, 8))
    if parent is None:
        inits.extend(make_sparse_inits(n, rng, 2))

    clean = []
    for a in inits:
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        if len(a) == n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            clean.append(a)
    return clean


def lse_score_grad(x, beta):
    """Log-sum-exp smoothed score and gradient."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(x)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = fast_conv(x)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    maxs = float(np.max(scaled))
    exps = np.exp(scaled - maxs)
    sumps = float(exps.sum())
    if sumps <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        lse_l = cmax
    else:
        p = exps / sumps
        lse_l = cmax + (maxs + float(np.log(sumps))) / beta

    rev = x[::-1]
    M2 = 3 * n - 2
    M2f = 1 << max(1, (M2 - 1).bit_length())
    RA = np.fft.rfft(rev, M2f)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M2f)
    conv_res = np.fft.irfft(RA * P, M2f)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = 2.0 * conv_res

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    score = 2.0 * n * lse_l / S2
    grad = (2.0 * n) * (g * S - 2.0 * lse_l) / S3
    return float(score), grad


def weighted_score_grad(x, w):
    """Score for a fixed nonnegative weight vector over convolution lags."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(x)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = fast_conv(x)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    wsum = float(w.sum())
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(c) / c.size
        wsum = 1.0
    w = w / wsum

    w_num = float(np.dot(w, c))
    score = 2.0 * n * w_num / (S * S)

    rev = x[::-1]
    M2 = 3 * n - 2
    M2f = 1 << max(1, (M2 - 1).bit_length())
    RA = np.fft.rfft(rev, M2f)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, M2f)
    conv_res = np.fft.irfft(RA * W, M2f)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = 2.0 * conv_res

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    grad = (2.0 * n) * (g * S - 2.0 * w_num) / S3
    return float(score), grad


def obj_general(x):
    """Dispatch objective to the current _OPT mode.  For scipy.minimize."""
    if _OPT['mode'] == 'weighted':
        sc, g = weighted_score_grad(x, _OPT['w'])
    else:
        sc, g = lse_score_grad(x, _OPT['beta'])
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = 1e10
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros_like(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))
    return float(sc), np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)


def cb_general(*args):
    """Stop L-BFGS when the caller's stage deadline is reached."""
    return time.monotonic() > _STATE['deadline']


def run_lbfgs(a0, mode, param, maxiter=3000, maxfun=15000, t_end=None):
    """Run L-BFGS-B for either the LSE or weighted objective."""
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        S = float(a.size)
    a = a / S

    _OPT['mode'] = mode
    if mode == 'weighted':
        _OPT['w'] = np.asarray(param, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        _OPT['beta'] = 1000.0
    else:
        _OPT['beta'] = float(param)
        _OPT['w'] = None

    if t_end is None:
        _STATE['deadline'] = np.inf
    else:
        _STATE['deadline'] = float(t_end)

    n = len(a)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            obj_general,
            a,
            method='L-BFGS-B',
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            callback=cb_general,
            options={
                'ftol': 1e-14,
                'gtol': 1e-11,
                'maxiter': int(maxiter),
                'maxfun': int(maxfun),
                'maxls': 20,
            }
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return a

    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return a
    return x / S


def waterfill_target(c, clip_ratio=0.98, floor=0.0):
    """
    Normalised convolution target obtained by clipping current peaks at
    clip_ratio*cmax and redistributing the missing mass to lower lags.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(c, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)
    c = c / s

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    R = max(1e-300, float(clip_ratio) * cmax)
    if R <= 0.0:
        return c

    q = np.minimum(c, R)
    rem = 1.0 - float(q.sum())
    if rem > 0.0:
        order = np.argsort(q)
        for i in order:
            if rem <= 1e-15:
                break
            cap = R - q[i]
            if cap > 0.0:
                d = cap if cap < rem else rem
                q[i] += d
                rem -= d
    if rem > 1e-14:
        q = q + rem / float(q.size)
    if floor > 0.0:
        q = q + float(floor)

    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        q = np.ones_like(c, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)
    else:
        q /= s
    return q


def gs_target_array(a0, q, t_end, n_iters=250, alpha_init=0.6, alpha_final=0.05, score_every=5):
    """
    GS/Fienup-style iteration enforcing a desired convolution target q in the
    frequency domain.  q must have length 2n-1 and sum 1.
    """
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a

    Lsup = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1 << max(1, (Lsup - 1).bit_length())
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if q.size != Lsup:
        tmp = np.zeros(Lsup, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(q.size, Lsup)
        tmp[:m] = q[:m]
        q = tmp
    qs = float(q.sum())
    if qs <= 0.0:
        q = np.ones(Lsup, dtype=np.float64) / float(Lsup)
    else:
        q = q / qs

    qp = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    qp[:Lsup] = q
    T = np.fft.fft(qp)
    T_mag = np.maximum(np.abs(T), 1e-30)
    T_phase = np.angle(T)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)

    for it in range(n_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > t_end:
            break
        prog = min(1.0, it / max(1, n_iters - 1))
        alpha = alpha_init + (alpha_final - alpha_init) * prog

        A = np.fft.fft(a, M)
        A_mag = np.maximum(np.abs(A), 1e-30)
        A_phase = np.angle(A)

        new_mag = (A_mag ** (1.0 - alpha)) * (T_mag ** (0.5 * alpha))
        new_phase = (1.0 - alpha) * A_phase + (0.5 * alpha) * T_phase
        A_new = new_mag * np.exp(1j * new_phase)
        a_new = np.fft.ifft(A_new, M).real[:n]
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        sn = float(a_new.sum())
        if sn <= 1e-12:
            break
        a_new = a_new / sn
        a = a_new

        if (it % score_every == 0) or (it == n_iters - 1):
            sc = compute_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()
            if (not np.isfinite(sc)) or sc > best_sc * 1.5:
                a = best.copy()

    sc_final = compute_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best = a.copy()
    return best


def gs_adaptive_waterfill(a0, t_end, n_iters_max=250, recompute_every=18,
                          clip_start=0.92, clip_end=0.99,
                          alpha_init=0.55, alpha_final=0.04):
    """GS iteration with an adaptively water-filled convolution target."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)
    if n == 0:
        return best

    Lsup = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1 << max(1, (Lsup - 1).bit_length())

    c = fast_conv(a)
    prog = 0.0
    clip = clip_start
    q = waterfill_target(c, clip_ratio=clip, floor=0.02)
    qp = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    qp[:Lsup] = q
    T = np.fft.fft(qp)
    T_mag = np.maximum(np.abs(T), 1e-30)
    T_phase = np.angle(T)

    for it in range(n_iters_max):
        if time.monotonic() > t_end:
            break
        prog = min(1.0, it / max(1, n_iters_max - 1))
        alpha = alpha_init + (alpha_final - alpha_init) * prog
        clip = clip_start + (clip_end - clip_start) * prog

        if it % recompute_every == 0:
            c = fast_conv(a)
            q = waterfill_target(c, clip_ratio=clip, floor=0.02)
            qp = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
            qp[:Lsup] = q
            T = np.fft.fft(qp)
            T_mag = np.maximum(np.abs(T), 1e-30)
            T_phase = np.angle(T)

        A = np.fft.fft(a, M)
        A_mag = np.maximum(np.abs(A), 1e-30)
        A_phase = np.angle(A)

        new_mag = (A_mag ** (1.0 - alpha)) * (T_mag ** (0.5 * alpha))
        new_phase = (1.0 - alpha) * A_phase + (0.5 * alpha) * T_phase
        A_new = new_mag * np.exp(1j * new_phase)
        a_new = np.fft.ifft(A_new, M).real[:n]
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        sn = float(a_new.sum())
        if sn <= 1e-12:
            break
        a_new = a_new / sn
        a = a_new

        if it % 5 == 0 or it == n_iters_max - 1:
            sc = compute_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()
            if (not np.isfinite(sc)) or sc > best_sc * 1.5:
                a = best.copy()

    sc_final = compute_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best = a.copy()
    return best


def iterative_reweighting_best(a0, t_end, max_outers=30, chunk_s=90.0):
    """
    Dual-ish iterative reweighting: use soft peak weights and solve the
    corresponding weighted quadratic ratio with L-BFGS.
    """
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return best, best_sc

    for outer in range(max_outers):
        if time.monotonic() > t_end:
            break
        c = fast_conv(a)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
            break

        temp = cmax * (0.30 * (0.72 ** outer) + 0.025)
        w = np.exp((c - cmax) / max(temp, 1e-300))
        w = w + 1e-8
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / ws
        w = 0.95 * w + 0.05 / float(2 * n - 1)
        w = w / float(w.sum())

        inner_end = min(t_end, time.monotonic() + chunk_s)
        if inner_end <= time.monotonic():
            break
        a2 = run_lbfgs(a, 'weighted', w, maxiter=800, maxfun=4000, t_end=inner_end)
        a2 = normalize_sequence(a2)
        sc = compute_score(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a2.copy()
            print(f"    IR outer={outer}: {sc:.9f}")
        a = a2
        if sc > best_sc * 1.10:
            a = best.copy()

    return best, best_sc


def try_lp_step(a, delta, best_sc):
    """Line search over an LP-proposed additive step."""
    best = a.copy()
    bs = best_sc
    alphas = (1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01)
    for alpha in alphas:
        a_new = a + alpha * delta
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)):
            continue
        mn = float(np.min(a_new))
        if mn < -1e-8:
            continue
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        S = float(a_new.sum())
        if S <= 0.01:
            continue
        a_new = a_new / S
        sc = compute_score(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bs * (1.0 - 1e-9):
            bs = sc
            best = a_new.copy()
            break
    return best, bs


def lp_polish(a0, max_rows=120, max_rounds=3, rho=0.1, t_end=None):
    """
    LP linearised polish.  At each step, choose a spatial sample of near-maximal
    convolution lags and solve:
        max eta
        subject to  L_k delta + eta <= cmax - c_k   for sampled peaks,
                    sum(delta)=0, box(delta), eta>=0.
    Then line-search the additive update exactly.
    """
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)
    if n == 0:
        return best, best_sc

    for _round in range(max_rounds):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if t_end is not None and now > t_end:
            break

        c = fast_conv(a)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
            break
        Lsup = len(c)

        K = int(max_rows)
        if n > 10000:
            K = min(K, 80)
        elif n > 5500:
            K = min(K, 130)
        K = max(20, min(K, Lsup))

        order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        top_n = max(10, min(K // 2, Lsup))
        top = order[:top_n]

        high_mask = c >= 0.965 * cmax
        high_idx = np.where(high_mask)[0]
        sample_n = max(1, K - top_n)
        if high_idx.size > sample_n:
            step = max(1, high_idx.size // sample_n)
            sampled = high_idx[::step][:sample_n]
            active = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, sampled)))[:K]
        else:
            active = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, high_idx)))[:K]

        if active.size < K:
            extra = order[active.size:K]
            if extra.size:
                active = np.unique(np.concatenate((active, extra)))[:K]

        Klen = int(active.size)
        if Klen <= 0:
            break

        A_ub = np.zeros((Klen, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.empty(Klen, dtype=np.float64)
        slack = 1e-10 * cmax + 1e-15

        for ii, kk in enumerate(active):
            kk = int(kk)
            ist = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            ied = min(kk, n - 1)
            A_ub[ii, ist:ied + 1] = 2.0 * a[kk - ist: kk - ied - 1: -1]
            A_ub[ii, -1] = 1.0
            b_ub[ii] = max(0.0, cmax - c[kk]) + slack

        A_eq = np.ones(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[-1] = 0.0
        c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj[-1] = -1.0

        scale = float(a.mean())
        lo = -rho * a
        hi = rho * (a + 0.5 * scale) + 1e-12

        bounds = []
        for i in range(n):
            bounds.append((float(lo[i]), float(hi[i])))
        bounds.append((0.0, float(0.30 * cmax)))

        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=[0.0],
                bounds=bounds,
                method='highs',
                options={
                    'primal_tolerance': 1e-10,
                    'dual_tolerance': 1e-12,
                    'gap_tol': 1e-10,
                    'maxiter': 20000
                }
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if not res.success:
            break

        eta = float(res.x[-1])
        delta = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        if eta <= 1e-12 or not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
            break

        a_new, sc_new = try_lp_step(a, delta, best_sc)
        if sc_new < best_sc:
            best = a_new
            best_sc = sc_new
            a = best
            print(f"    LP polish: {best_sc:.9f}")
        else:
            break

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search for a low-scoring non-negative coefficient sequence."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)

    if total <= 80.0:
        deadline = t0 + max(1.0, total - 3.0)
    else:
        deadline = t0 + max(30.0, total - 45.0)

    parent = None
    parent_score = np.inf
    try:
        if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 16 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                parent = p
                ps = compute_score(p)
                if np.isfinite(ps):
                    parent_score = ps
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        parent_score = np.inf

    # Choose a working length.
    cand_ns = []
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        L = int(parent.size)
        cand_ns.append(L)
        cand_ns.append(8192)
        if 0 < L < 8192:
            cand_ns.append(min(8192, L + 1000))
        cand_ns.append(6962)
    else:
        cand_ns.extend([8192, 6962, 7500])

    unique_ns = []
    for nn in cand_ns:
        if 128 <= int(nn) <= 12000 and int(nn) not in unique_ns:
            unique_ns.append(int(nn))
    if not unique_ns:
        unique_ns = [8192]

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    chosen_n = unique_ns[0]

    for nn in unique_ns:
        if parent is not None:
            a = interpolate_sequence(parent, nn)
        else:
            a = make_fourier_inits(nn, rng, 1)[0]
        sc = compute_score(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        # Mild preference for larger n when quality is nearly the same.
        adj = sc - 0.004 * (float(nn) / 8192.0)
        if adj < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a
            chosen_n = nn

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(chosen_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(chosen_n)
        best_sc = compute_score(best_a)

    n = int(chosen_n)
    best_a = normalize_sequence(best_a)
    best_sc = compute_score(best_a)
    last_improve = time.monotonic()

    usable = deadline - t0
    stage1 = t0 + 0.07 * usable
    stage2 = t0 + 0.25 * usable
    stage3 = t0 + 0.52 * usable
    stage4 = t0 + 0.86 * usable

    print(f"Start n={n}, score={best_sc:.9f}, parent={parent_score if np.isfinite(parent_score) else float('inf'):.9f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: initialisation and plateau GS.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        inits = build_initial_candidates(n, parent, rng)
        scored = []
        for idx, a in enumerate(inits):
            sc = compute_score(a)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = normalize_sequence(a)
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                print(f"  init {idx}: {best_sc:.9f}")
            scored.append((sc, idx, normalize_sequence(a)))

        scored.sort()
        top = []
        for item in scored:
            top.append(item)
            if len(top) > 5:
                break
        for _, _, a_init in top:
            if time.monotonic() > max(t0, stage1 - 20.0):
                break
            target_score = max(1.05, min(2.4, best_sc * 0.985))
            q = plateau_target_array(n, target_score)
            a2 = gs_target_array(a_init, q, t_end=stage1, n_iters=260)
            sc2 = compute_score(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_a = a2.copy()
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                print(f"  plateau GS: {best_sc:.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"After Phase 1: {best_sc:.9f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: LSE L-BFGS.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        betas = [500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0, 25000.0, 70000.0, 160000.0]
        bi = 0
        while time.monotonic() < stage3:
            beta = betas[bi % len(betas)]
            if bi >= len(betas):
                beta *= (1.25 ** ((bi // len(betas)) + 1))
            inner_end = min(stage3, time.monotonic() + 95.0)
            if inner_end <= time.monotonic():
                break
            a2 = run_lbfgs(best_a, 'lse', beta, maxiter=2500, maxfun=12000, t_end=inner_end)
            a2 = normalize_sequence(a2)
            sc2 = compute_score(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_a = a2.copy()
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                print(f"  LSE beta={beta:.0f}: {best_sc:.9f}")
            bi += 1
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"After Phase 2: {best_sc:.9f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: alternating IR, LP polish, adaptive GS.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        while time.monotonic() < stage4:
            now = time.monotonic()

            if now < stage4 - 170.0:
                a2, sc2 = iterative_reweighting_best(
                    best_a, t_end=min(stage4, now + 130.0),
                    max_outers=8, chunk_s=90.0
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()

            if time.monotonic() < stage4 - 80.0:
                a2, sc2 = lp_polish(
                    best_a, max_rows=130, max_rounds=2, rho=0.10,
                    t_end=min(stage4, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()

            if time.monotonic() < stage4 - 35.0:
                a2 = gs_adaptive_waterfill(
                    best_a, t_end=min(stage4, time.monotonic() + 75.0),
                    n_iters_max=180
                )
                sc2 = compute_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    print(f"  adaptive GS: {best_sc:.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"After Phase 3: {best_sc:.9f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: cycling until near deadline.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        mode = 0
        lse_betas = [3000.0, 12000.0, 45000.0]
        gp_target_score = max(1.05, min(2.2, best_sc * 0.99))

        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            now = time.monotonic()

            # Perturbed restart if stuck for a while.
            if now - last_improve > 330.0:
                a_try = perturbed_restart(best_a, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.025, 0.065)))
                a_try = run_lbfgs(a_try, 'lse', 10000.0, maxiter=600, maxfun=3000,
                                  t_end=min(deadline, now + 85.0))
                a_try = normalize_sequence(a_try)
                sc_try = compute_score(a_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_try
                    best_a = a_try.copy()
                    print(f"  restart: {best_sc:.9f}")
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                mode += 1
                continue

            if mode % 5 == 0:
                beta = lse_betas[(mode // 5) % len(lse_betas)]
                a2 = run_lbfgs(best_a, 'lse', beta, maxiter=1800, maxfun=9000,
                               t_end=min(deadline, now + 85.0))
                a2 = normalize_sequence(a2)
                sc2 = compute_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    print(f"  cycle LSE beta={beta:.0f}: {best_sc:.9f}")

            elif mode % 5 == 1:
                a2, sc2 = lp_polish(best_a, max_rows=110, max_rounds=2, rho=0.12,
                                    t_end=min(deadline, now + 65.0))
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    print(f"  cycle LP: {best_sc:.9f}")

            elif mode % 5 == 2:
                a2, sc2 = iterative_reweighting_best(
                    best_a, t_end=min(deadline, now + 95.0),
                    max_outers=5, chunk_s=60.0
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    print(f"  cycle IR: {best_sc:.9f}")

            elif mode % 5 == 3:
                a2 = gs_adaptive_waterfill(best_a, t_end=min(deadline, now + 65.0),
                                           n_iters_max=120)
                sc2 = compute_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    print(f"  cycle adaptive GS: {best_sc:.9f}")

            else:
                gp_target_score = max(1.05, min(2.2, best_sc * 0.992))
                q = plateau_target_array(n, gp_target_score)
                a2 = gs_target_array(best_a, q, t_end=min(deadline, now + 55.0),
                                     n_iters=110)
                sc2 = compute_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    print(f"  cycle plateau GS: {best_sc:.9f}")

            mode += 1
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final polish and cross-length check.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
            a2, sc2 = lp_polish(best_a, max_rows=150, max_rounds=2, rho=0.05,
                                t_end=deadline - 10.0)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_a = a2.copy()
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                print(f"  final LP: {best_sc:.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
            cur_len = len(best_a)
            for nn in unique_ns:
                if nn == cur_len or time.monotonic() > deadline - 6.0:
                    continue
                a2 = interpolate_sequence(best_a, nn)
                sc2 = compute_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    print(f"  cross-length {nn}: {best_sc:.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if parent is not None and np.isfinite(parent_score) and parent_score < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best_a = parent.copy()
        best_sc = parent_score
        print("  using original parent as fallback")

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if best_a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
        best_a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0

    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(best_a.sum())
    if S <= 0.01:
        best_a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        best_a = best_a / S

    out = []
    for x in best_a:
        if x < 0.0:
            x = 0.0
        elif x > 1000.0:
            x = 1000.0
        out.append(float(x))

    print(f"INTERNAL FINAL: score={best_sc:.9f}, n={len(out)}, time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}/{total:.1f}")

    try:
        if time.monotonic() < t0 + total - 3.0:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            print(f"VERIFIED FINAL: {verified:.9f}")
            if not np.isfinite(verified) and parent is not None and np.isfinite(parent_score):
                out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(x)))) for x in parent]
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out
```
